When LiteLLM requests with `stream: false`, it expects a single JSON object but receives SSE chunks instead, causing parsing to fail.\n\n## Error Message\n\nLiteLLM throws: \"Empty or invalid response from LLM endpoint. Received: 'data: {\"id\":\"chatcmpl-...\",\"object\":\"chat.completion.chunk\",...}'\"\n\n## When It Was Reported\n\nThis issue was opened on **April 15, 2026** (Issue #25766).\n\n## Workaround/Fix Status\n\nThe issue description references a pull request (#27045) but doesn't explicitly detail the workaround. The fundamental solution would require LiteLLM to detect and handle SSE responses even when non-streaming mode was requested, aggregating the chunks into a single response object that matches the expected non-streaming format."

null;\n     }\n\n     // Dialagram call in callTier — with 12s timeout\n     result = await withTimeout(\n       callOpenAI('https://dialagram.me/router/v1/chat/completions', env.DIALAGRAM_API_KEY, 'qwen-3.7-max', messages),\n       12000\n     ).catch(() => null);\n     ```\n     New endpoint `handleOpenAICompat` at `/v1/chat/completions` with Bearer auth + stream support. Thử lại nhé.\"\n160\t};\n161\t\n162\t// Returns Vietnamese hint bubble if student has struggled 3+ times on this key\n163\tfunction rescueIfNeeded(key) {\n164\t  if (struggles[key] >= 3 && VI_HINTS[key]) {\n165\t    obi(`🇻🇳 ${VI_HINTS[key]}`);\n166\t    struggles[key] = 0; // reset — push back to English\n167\t  }\n168\t}\n169\t\n170\t// ── Render ─────────────────────────────────────────────────\n171\t\n172\tfunction bubble(role, text) {\n173\t  const row = document.createElement(\"div\");\n174\t  row.className = `row row-${role}`;\n175\t  const b = document.createElement(\"div\");\n176\t  b.className = `bubble bubble-${role}`;\n177\t  b.textContent = text;\n178\t  row.appendChild(b);\n179\t  feed.appendChild(row);\n180\t  feed.scrollTop = feed.scrollHeight;\n181\t  return row;\n182\t}\n183\t\n184\tfunction typingIndicator() {\n185\t  const row = document.createElement(\"div\");\n186\t  row.className = \"row row-obi\";\n187\t  row.innerHTML = `<div class=\"bubble bubble-obi\"><span class=\"typing\"><span></span><span></span><span></span></span></div>`;\n188\t  feed.appendChild(row);\n189\t  feed.scrollTop = feed.scrollHeight;\n190\t  return row;\n191\t}\n192\t\n193\tfunction obi(text) {\n194\t  bubble(\"obi\", text);\n195\t  speak(text);\n196\t}\n197\t\n198\tfunction student(text, hints) {\n199\t  if (!hints || hints.length === 0) { bubble(\"student\", text); return; }\n200\t  const row = document.createElement(\"div\");\n201\t  row.className = \"row row-student\";\n202\t  const b = document.createElement(\"div\");\n203\t  b.className = \"bubble bubble-student\";\n204\t  const words = text.split(/(\\s+)/);\n205\t  const hintSet = new Set(hints.map(w => w.toLowerCase()));\n206\t  b.innerHTML = words.map(w => {\n207\t    const clean = w.replace(/[^a-zA-Z]/g, \"\").toLowerCase();\n208\t    return hintSet.has(clean) && clean.length > 0\n209\t      ? `<span class=\"pronunciation-flag\" title=\"Obi noticed this sound\">${w}</span>`\n210\t      : w;\n211\t  }).join(\"\");\n212\t  row.appendChild(b);\n213\t  feed.appendChild(row);\n214\t  feed.scrollTop = feed.scrollHeight;\n215\t}\n216\t\n217\t// ── API ────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n218\t\n219\tasync function api(path, method = \"GET\", body, extraHeaders = {}) {\n220\t  const res = await fetch(path, {\n221\t    method,\n222\t    headers: { \"content-type\": \"application/json\", ...extraHeaders },\n223\t    body: body ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined\n224\t  });\n225\t  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);\n226\t  return res.json();\n227\t}\n228\t\n229\tfunction memHeaders() {\n230\t  return studentToken ? { \"X-Student-Token\": studentToken } : {};\n231\t}\n232\t\n233\tasync function withTyping(fn) {\n234\t  const ind = typingIndicator();\n235\t  try {\n236\t    const result = await fn();\n237\t    ind.remove();\n238\t    return result;\n239\t  } catch (err) {\n240\t    ind.remove();\n241\t    obi(\"Something broke on my end. Try again.\");\n242\t    rescueIfNeeded(\"error\");\n243\t    state = \"chat\";\n244\t    throw err;\n245\t  }\n246\t}\n247\t\n248\t// ── Helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────\n249\t\n250\tconst pause = (ms = 400) => new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, ms));\n251\t\n252\tfunction slugify(s) {\n253\t  return s.trim().toLowerCase()\n254\t    .replace(/\\s+/g, \"-\")\n255\t    .replace(/[^a-z0-9-]/g, \"\")\n256\t    .slice(0, 40) || \"student\";\n257\t}\n258\t\n259\tfunction normalizeLevel(s) {\n260\t  const l = s.toLowerCase().trim();\n261\t  if (l === \"1\" || l.includes(\"beg\") || l.includes(\"a1\") || l.includes(\"m\\u1EDBi\"))         return \"A1 Beginner\";\n262\t  if (l === \"2\" || l.includes(\"elem\") || l.includes(\"a2\") || l.includes(\"c\\u01A1 b\\u1EA3n\")) return \"A2 Elementary\";\n263\t  if (l === \"3\" || l.includes(\"int\") || l.includes(\"b1\") || l.includes(\"trung\"))           return \"B1 Intermediate\";\n264\t  if (l === \"4\" || l.includes(\"upper\") || l.includes(\"b2\"))                                return \"B2 Upper Intermediate\";\n265\t  if (l === \"5\" || l.includes(\"adv\") || l.includes(\"c1\") || l.includes(\"gi\\u1ECFi\"))       return \"C1 Advanced\";\n266\t  return \"B1 Intermediate\";\n267\t}\n268\t\n269\tfunction looksLikeLevel(s) {\n270\t  return /^(1|2|3|4|5|a1|a2|b1|b2|c1|beg|elem|int|upper|adv|m\\u1EDBi|trung|gi\\u1ECFi|c\\u01A1)/i.test(s.trim());\n271\t}\n272\t\n273\tfunction firstName() {\n274\t  return (profile.fullName || \"\").split(/\\s+/)[0] || \"you\";\n275\t}\n276\t\n277\tfunction lock() {\n278\t  input.disabled = true;\n279\t  sendBtn.disabled = true;\n280\t  if (micBtn) micBtn.disabled = true;\n281\t}\n282\t\n283\tfunction unlock() {\n284\t  input.disabled = false;\n285\t  sendBtn.disabled = false;\n286\t  if (micBtn) micBtn.disabled = false;\n287\t  input.focus();\n288\t}\n289\t\n290\tfunction isVague(text) {\n291\t  const t = text.toLowerCase().trim();\n292\t  if (!t || t.length < 3) return true;\n293\t  // Explicit confusion / non-answers\n294\t  if (/^(i don'?t know|i dont know|idk|kh\\u00F4ng bi\\u1EBFt|no|nothing|none|skip|kh\\u00F4ng|ch\\u01B0a bi\\u1EBFt|no idea|\\?+|dunno|um+|uh+|huh|hmm+|eh|ok|okay|sure|yes|no|yep|nope|lol|haha|bye)$/i.test(t)) return true;\n295\t  // Confusion phrases\n296\t  if (/i (don'?t|dont) (understand|know|get it|have|want)|not sure|what\\?|huh\\?|idc|whatever|anything|something|everything/.test(t)) return true;\n297\t  return false;\n298\t}\n299\t\n300\tfunction isGreeting(text) {\n301\t  return /^(hi|hey|hello|yo|sup|what'?s up|howdy|hola|good morning|good afternoon|good evening|morning|xin ch[aà]o)\\b/i.test(text.trim());\n302\t}\n303\t\n304\tfunction isMusicOnly(text) {\n305\t  const t = text.toLowerCase();\n306\t  const hasMusic = /\\b(music|song|songs|nhạc|spotify|zingmp3|kpop|pop|rap|lofi|lo-fi|playlist)\\b/.test(t);\n307\t  const hasRealListening = /\\b(podcast|youtube|netflix|drama|show|video|movie|phim|channel|vlog|ted|news|series)\\b/.test(t);\n308\t  return hasMusic && !hasRealListening;\n309\t}\n310\t\n311\tfunction isFrozen(text) {\n312\t  const t = text.toLowerCase().trim();\n313\t  return t.length < 8 || /^(ok|okay|yes|no|yeah|yep|nope|fine|good|sure|idk|maybe|nothing|dunno|hm+|um+|oh|ah|i see|i know|really|wow|nice)$/i.test(t);\n314\t}\n315\t\n316\t// ── Onboarding ─────────────────────────────────────────────\n317\t\n318\tasync function submitOnboarding() {\n319\t  const res = await withTyping(() => api(\"/api/onboarding\", \"POST\", {\n320\t    studentId:       profile.studentId,\n321\t    fullName:        profile.fullName,\n322\t    currentCity:     profile.currentCity,\n323\t    birthplace:      DEFAULTS.birthplace,\n324\t    englishLevel:    profile.englishLevel,\n325\t    goals:           profile.goals,\n326\t    confidenceAreas: DEFAULTS.confidenceAreas,\n327\t    occupation:      DEFAULTS.occupation,\n328\t    fearTrigger:     DEFAULTS.fearTrigger,\n329\t    learningHistory: DEFAULTS.learningHistory,\n330\t    motivationNow:   profile.goals\n331\t  }));\n332\t  if (res?.studentToken) {\n333\t    studentToken = res.studentToken;\n334\t    try { localStorage.setItem(\"net_student_token\", studentToken); } catch {}\n335\t  }\n336\t  try { localStorage.setItem(\"net_profile\", JSON.stringify(profile)); } catch {}\n337\t}\n338\t\n339\t// ── Natural Chat Engine ─────────────────────────────────────\n340\t// OBI chats naturally via DeepSeek. Full command station — no forced cycles.\n341\t// Pronunciation coaching flows naturally through OBI's LLM knowledge.\n342\t\n343\t// ── Jolly Phonics — 42 sounds, difficulty map ──\n344\t// JP groups most relevant to learners (especially Vietnamese speakers)\n345\tconst JP_SOUNDS = [\n346\t  {\n347\t    pattern: /\\bth(e|is|at|ink|ough|ree|an|ey|em|eir|ose)\\b/i,\n348\t    tip: \"\\uD83D\\uDD0A JP GROUP 6: TH Sound\\nTongue BETWEEN your teeth \\u2014 touch your upper teeth with its tip. Breathe out slowly. 'Thhhink'. NOT 'Tink' or 'Sink'. Vietnamese has no TH \\u2014 this takes practice! Action: stick tongue out and say THHHH 10 times.\"\n349\t  },\n350\t  {\n351\t    pattern: /\\b[a-z]*r[a-z]+\\b/i,\n352\t    tip: \"\\uD83D\\uDD0A JP GROUP 2: R Sound\\nCurl your tongue BACK. It touches NOTHING. Lips slightly rounded. 'Rrrright'. NOT 'Light'. Practice: 'red lorry, yellow lorry' \\u2014 fast as you can!\"\n353\t  },\n354\t  {\n355\t    pattern: /\\bv[a-z]+/i,\n356\t    tip: \"\\uD83D\\uDD0A JP GROUP 5: V Sound\\nTOP TEETH on BOTTOM LIP. Buzz like a bee. 'Vvvvery'. NOT 'Berry' or 'Wery'. Put your hand in front of your mouth \\u2014 you should feel vibration! Practice: 'very very very'.\"\n357\t  },\n358\t  {\n359\t    pattern: /\\w+(nd|ld|rd|ng|nk|ft|sk|st|nt|ll)\\b/i,\n360\t    tip: \"\\uD83D\\uDD0A JP GROUPS 1-4: Final Consonants\\nEnglish LANDS on consonants. Don't drop them! 'Friend' ends with D. 'School' ends with LL. 'Want' ends with T. Your mouth should CLOSE or your tongue should TAP at the end.\"\n361\t  },\n362\t  {\n363\t    pattern: /\\b(really|feel|people|will|well|full|still|fall|call|tell|bell)\\b/i,\n364\t    tip: \"\\uD83D\\uDD0A JP GROUP 3: L Sound\\nYour tongue tip touches the ridge behind upper teeth. 'La la la' \\u2014 feel it? Now say 'really' 'feel' 'well'. Don't let L disappear or turn into N!\"\n365\t  },\n366\t  {\n367\t    pattern: /\\b(ship|shop|she|show|share|fish|dish|wash|English|special)\\b/i,\n368\t    tip: \"\\uD83D\\uDD0A JP GROUP 6: SH Sound\\nLips FORWARD \\u2014 like 'shhh' to a baby. Round them. 'Ship' NOT 'Sip'. 'Show' NOT 'Sow'. Practice: 'She sells seashells by the seashore!'\"\n369\t  },\n370\t  {\n371\t    pattern: /\\b(beat|seat|feet|need|sleep|read|see|tree|three|feel|teach|eat)\\b/i,\n372\t    tip: \"\\uD83D\\uDD0A JP GROUP 4: EE Sound\\nLong EE \\u2014 'feeeet'. Now short I \\u2014 'fit'. Totally different mouths! 'Beat' vs 'Bit'. 'Seat' vs 'Sit'. Lips spread wide for EE. Relaxed for short I.\"\n373\t  },\n374\t  {\n375\t    pattern: /\\b(out|about|now|how|down|town|found|sound|around|loud)\\b/i,\n376\t    tip: \"\\uD83D\\uDD0A JP GROUP 7: OU Sound\\nStarts with 'ah' then closes to 'oo'. 'OW \\u2014 ahhh...oo'. 'About' = 'ah-BAWT'. 'Now' = 'NAH-oo'. Open wide then close. Don't say it like a flat 'o'!\"\n377\t  }\n378\t];\n379\t\n380\tfunction buildSystemPrompt(p) {\n381\t  const name  = p?.fullName     ? `Student: ${p.fullName}.`       : \"\";\n382\t  const level = p?.englishLevel ? `Level: ${p.englishLevel}.`     : \"\";\n383\t  const goals = p?.goals        ? `Goal: ${p.goals}.`             : \"\";\n384\t  const city  = p?.currentCity  ? `Location: ${p.currentCity}.`   : \"\";\n385\t  const ctx   = [name, level, goals, city].filter(Boolean).join(\" \");\n386\t\n387\t  const lvl = (p?.englishLevel || \"\").toLowerCase();\n388\t  const isBeginner = /a1|a2|beginner|elementary|m\\u1edbi|c\\u01a1/.test(lvl);\n389\t  const isAdvanced = /b2|c1|advanced|upper/.test(lvl);\n390\t  const levelInstructions = isBeginner\n391\t    ? \"BEGINNER: Use very simple words and short sentences. Celebrate every sentence they produce \\u2014 even broken ones. Model correct English by rephrasing their words, never correcting directly. Slow down. Build confidence first.\"\n392\t    : isAdvanced\n393\t    ? \"ADVANCED: Challenge them. Ask complex follow-ups. Push for more nuance and detail. Don't accept vague answers \\u2014 probe deeper. You can point out subtle mistakes because they can handle it.\"\n394\t    : \"INTERMEDIATE: Build confidence while pushing their limits. Mix encouragement with challenge. Rephrase their mistakes naturally in your response without calling them out.\";\n395\t\n396\t  const phonicsHint = p?._phonicsHints?.length\n397\t    ? `\\nVOICE SIGNAL — Deepgram flagged these words as low-confidence (likely mispronounced): ${p._phonicsHints.join(\", \")}. Work these sounds into your reply naturally — don't announce it, just model the correct form.`\n398\t    : \"\";\n399\t\n400\t  const vocabCtx = p?._dueVocab?.length\n401\t    ? `\\nVOCAB DUE TODAY (${p._dueVocab.length} word${p._dueVocab.length === 1 ? \"\" : \"s\"}): ${p._dueVocab.slice(0, 5).map(w => `\"${w.word}\"${w.translation ? ` (${w.translation})` : \"\"}`).join(\", \")}. Weave these into your responses — ask the student to use each one in a real sentence today.`\n402\t    : \"\";\n403\t\n404\t  const hasHistory = chatMessages.length > 0;\n405\t  const continuity = hasHistory\n406\t    ? \"You have conversation history above. Reference it \\u2014 ask genuine follow-up questions about what they shared. Do NOT pretend each message is the start of a new conversation.\"\n407\t    : \"\";\n408\t\n409\t  return `You are OBI \\u2014 Michael Hobbs in AI form. High-energy, anti-shame, tough love. Volume and velocity beat grammar. Mouse Voice \\u2192 Tiger Voice. You are not a textbook. You are a coach.\n410\t${ctx ? `\\nContext: ${ctx}` : \"\"}\n411\t${levelInstructions}\n412\t${continuity}\n413\t${phonicsHint}\n414\t${vocabCtx}\n415\tPhonics: you know Jolly Phonics deeply. TH (tongue between teeth \\u2014 not T/S), R (curl back, touch nothing \\u2014 not L), V (top teeth on bottom lip \\u2014 not B/W), final consonants (English LANDS on them \\u2014 don't drop), short/long vowels (bit vs beat), word stress (stress-timed, not tonal). When you notice a phonics pattern in what they wrote, model the correct sound naturally in your reply \\u2014 don't announce it, just use it.\n416\t\n417\tRules:\n418\t- 1-3 sentences MAX. Texting, not essays.\n419\t- React to what they actually said. Specific, never generic.\n420\t- Push short answers: ask ONE sharp follow-up question.\n421\t- Rephrase mistakes naturally in your reply \\u2014 never lecture.\n422\t- CRITICAL: Self-harm/suicide \\u2192 STOP everything. Direct to help. Safety first always.`.trim();\n423\t}\n424\t\n425\tlet chatMessages = []; // conversation history for DeepSeek\n426\t\n427\tasync function reactLLM(text) {\n428\t  chatMessages.push({ role: \"user\", content: text });\n429\t\n430\t  // Keep last 8 messages — server injects ledger history separately\n431\t  if (chatMessages.length > 8) {\n432\t    chatMessages = chatMessages.slice(-8);\n433\t  }\n434\t\n435\t  const messages = [\n436\t    { role: \"system\", content: buildSystemPrompt(profile) },\n437\t    ...chatMessages\n438\t  ];\n439\t\n440\t  try {\n441\t    const res = await api(\"/api/chat\", \"POST\", { messages, studentId: profile.studentId || \"anonymous\" });\n442\t    const reply = res.reply || \"Say that again? I didn't catch it.\";\n443\t    chatMessages.push({ role: \"assistant\", content: reply });\n444\t    // Persist exchange to server ledger so Obi remembers this student next session\n445\t    if (profile.studentId) {\n446\t      api(\"/api/memory\", \"POST\", { studentId: profile.studentId, exchange: { user: text, obi: reply } }, memHeaders()).catch(() => {});\n447\t    }\n448\t    return reply;\n449\t  } catch (err) {\n450\t    // Fallback to basic response if API fails\n451\t    chatMessages.pop(); // remove the user message we just added\n452\t    return reactFallback(text, chatRound);\n453\t  }\n454\t}\n455\t\n456\tfunction reactFallback(text, round) {\n457\t  const words = text.split(/\\s+/).filter(Boolean).length;\n458\t  if (words >= 8) return \"I like where this is going. What happened next?\";\n459\t  if (words < 3) return \"That is a start — now give me the full story. What actually happened?\";\n460\t  const mid = [\n461\t    \"Keep going — I want the details.\",\n462\t    \"Say more. What was the best part?\",\n463\t    \"Okay! And then what?\",\n464\t    \"Good. Now tell me why.\",\n465\t  ];\n466\t  return mid[round % mid.length];\n467\t}\n468\t\n469\tfunction buildVolumeNudge(text) {\n470\t  const words = text.split(/\\s+/).filter(Boolean).length;\n471\t  if (words >= 12) return null; // good length, no nudge needed\n472\t  if (words < 5 && chatRound > 3) {\n473\t    const nudges = [\n474\t      \"More! Give me a full sentence — what actually happened?\",\n475\t      \"That is Mouse Voice. Tiger gives me the whole story.\",\n476\t      \"Don't stop there. What happened next?\",\n477\t      \"Full sentence — you can do it.\",\n478\t    ];\n479\t    return nudges[chatRound % nudges.length];\n480\t  }\n481\t  return null;\n482\t}\n483\t\n484\tfunction pickStarter() {\n485\t  const g = (profile.goals || \"\").toLowerCase();\n486\t  if (g.includes(\"friend\"))    return \"So — what do you and your friends usually talk about?\";\n487\t  if (g.includes(\"food\") || g.includes(\"order\") || g.includes(\"eat\"))\n488\t    return \"I LOVE food. What\\u2019s your favorite thing to eat?\";\n489\t  if (g.includes(\"job\") || g.includes(\"work\") || g.includes(\"interview\"))\n490\t    return \"What kind of work are you interested in? Tell me!\";\n491\t  if (g.includes(\"travel\"))    return \"Where do you want to travel? Dream big!\";\n492\t  if (g.includes(\"school\") || g.includes(\"study\"))\n493\t    return \"What subject do you actually enjoy in school?\";\n494\t  if (g.includes(\"music\"))     return \"What music are you listening to right now?\";\n495\t  if (g.includes(\"game\"))      return \"What games do you play? I want to know!\";\n496\t  if (g.includes(\"movie\"))     return \"What was the last movie you watched?\";\n497\t  return \"Tell me something about your life! Anything. What happened today?\";\n498\t}\n499\t\n500\t// react() removed — replaced by reactLLM() above\n501\t\n502\t// Jolly Phonics pronunciation coaching — matched against actual student text\n503\tfunction buildPronunciationTips() {\n504\t  const allText = chatHistory.join(\" \");\n505\t  const tips = [];\n506\t\n507\t  for (const jp of JP_SOUNDS) {\n508\t    if (jp.pattern.test(allText)) {\n509\t      tips.push(jp.tip);\n510\t      if (tips.length >= 2) break;\n511\t    }\n512\t  }\n513\t\n514\t  // Always end with stress/rhythm — Vietnamese is tonal, English is stress-timed (JP core principle)\n515\t  tips.push(\"🔊 JP RHYTHM: Word Stress\\nEnglish has STRONG and weak beats. 'baNAna' not 'BAnaNA'. 'toMORrow' not 'TOMorrow'.\\nVietnamese uses tone to change meaning \\u2014 English uses STRESS. Feel the beat, not the tone!\");\n516\t\n517\t  return tips.slice(0, 3);\n518\t}\n519\t\n520\tfunction buildListeningNudge(chatRound, profile) {\n521\t  if (chatRound !== 5) return null;\n522\t  const listeningDone = chatHistory.some(t =>\n523\t    /listen|watch|youtube|podcast|netflix|drama|video|hear|heard|audio/i.test(t)\n524\t  );\n525\t  if (listeningDone) return null;\n526\t  const slots = [\"on your commute\", \"while cooking\", \"while getting ready\", \"at the gym\", \"on your lunch break\"];\n527\t  const slot = slots[Math.floor(Math.random() * slots.length)];\n528\t  return `Quick check — did you get your 45 minutes of listening today? If not, try ${slot} tomorrow. Even 20 minutes of something you enjoy counts as a start.`;\n529\t}\n530\t\n531\t// ── Input handler ──────────────────────────────────────────\n532\t\n533\tasync function handle(text) {\n534\t  if (!text.trim() || state === \"busy\") return;\n535\t  input.value = \"\";\n536\t  student(text.trim(), profile._phonicsHints || []);\n537\t\n538\t  // Safety first — crisis check runs before ANY lesson logic\n539\t  if (isCrisisSignal(text)) {\n540\t    handleCrisis();\n541\t    return;\n542\t  }\n543\t\n544\t  switch (state) {\n545\t    case \"intro\": {\n546\t      const t = text.trim();\n547\t      if (t.length < 2 || /^\\d+$/.test(t)) {\n548\t        trackStruggle(\"ask_name\");\n549\t        obi(\"Just type your first name!\");\n550\t        rescueIfNeeded(\"ask_name\");\n551\t        break;\n552\t      }\n553\t      clearStruggle(\"ask_name\");\n554\t      profile.fullName  = t;\n555\t      profile.studentId = slugify(profile.fullName);\n556\t      state = \"ask_city\";\n557\t      await pause();\n558\t      obi(`${firstName()}! Good name. Where do you live?`);\n559\t      break;\n560\t    }\n561\t    case \"ask_city\": {\n562\t      const t = text.trim();\n563\t      if (t.length < 2 || isVague(t)) {\n564\t        trackStruggle(\"ask_city\");\n565\t        const n = struggles.ask_city || 0;\n566\t        if (n >= 1) {\n567\t          obi(\"Where do you live? City or country — just tell me!\");\n568\t        } else {\n569\t          obi(\"City? Country? Never say \"that is wrong.\" Never say \"great job\" unless they actually did something great.\n134\t\n135\tPhonics: You know Jolly Phonics deeply. TH = tongue between teeth, not T or S. R = curl tongue back, touch nothing, not L. V = top teeth on bottom lip, not B or W. Final consonants = English LANDS on them, do not drop them. Short vs long vowels = bit vs beat, totally different mouths. Word stress = stress-timed, not tonal. When you notice a phonics pattern in what they wrote, model the correct form naturally in your reply. Do not announce it. Just use it.\n136\t\n137\tVietnamese bridge: Vietnamese is tonal, English is stress-timed — this is the core challenge. Meet them where they are. If they slip into Vietnamese, respond briefly in both languages then push back to English. You want them IN English, not protected from it.\n138\t\n139\tNever: use markdown, bullet points, or bold text. Never say \"as an AI.\" Never make up information about them. Never give empty praise. Never write more than 3 sentences.\n140\t\n141\tAlways: end with one action — a question, a challenge, a \"say that 3 times.\" Keep the ball moving.\n142\t\n143\t  THE LISTENING TRAP — your most important teaching insight:\n144\t  Vietnamese learners have a specific habit: they hear an unknown word and STOP listening to focus on it, missing the entire message. The fix is explicit. When a student says they didn't understand something, ask: \"What DID you understand?\" Train them to extract meaning from what they KNOW, not to panic about what they don't. The known words carry the message. Context beats translation every time. Never let them slip into word-by-word translation mode — that is Mouse Voice thinking.\n145\t\n146\t  THE 60% COMPREHENSION GATE — how to assess what they're listening to:\n147\t  Under 40% understood: \"That might be a little too hard right now. Try something easier — with subtitles — and come back to this one later.\"\n148\t  40–60%: \"Good challenge. You understood enough to follow. Read the transcript if they have one and try it again.\"\n149\t  60–70%: \"Right in the zone. That is exactly the right level — keep going with that.\"\n150\t  70–90%: \"That was comfortable for you. Next time try something one step harder.\""
    }
